# 影笔代理人 · 分镜（第 1–100 集）

## E01

> 4 段 11 切 · 共 51 秒 / 目标 60 秒

### E01-01 · S01（黄昏） · 11 秒 · 2 个分镜

> 走位：开场画师在画面右侧画架前，面朝左窗光；来访者随后从左侧门框入画，缩肩低头，两人相距约三步。

| 切 | 起点 | 秒 | 景别 | 运镜 | 配方 | 画面 | 人物 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| #1 | 0.00s | 5 | 全景 | Tracking Shot（跟拍） | — | 沈砚提着照心笔走到画架前，夕阳斜过木格窗，地板上他脚下干干净净没有影子。 楼梯口传来三下叩门声，沈砚合上速写本。 | 沈砚 |
| #2 | 5.00s | 6 | 中景 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 周明：「请问……这里是一心堂画室吗？」 周明推门进来，缩着肩，洗发白的工装袖口卷到小臂。 | 沈砚、周明 |

```text

integrated_multimodal_description:
[Shot 1] anchored to <Picture 1> the painter carries the heart-revealing brush to the easel in slanting orange sunset light, the worn floorboards beneath him completely bare of shadow; three knocks come from the dark stairwell and he closes the sketchbook; a tracking shot follows him from behind; the camera follows with a tracking shot.
[Shot 2] At 00:05.000, the camera cuts to <Picture 2>: the door opens and a hunched man in a faded work coat steps in, shoulders curled, sleeves rolled to his forearms, staring at the floor; he asks in a low voice while the painter behind the easel lifts his eyes; a static shot; the camera holds a static shot (S1), a weary male draftsman in his early thirties in a faded washed-out work coat, hoarse tight voice says <d>[Chinese]请问……这里是一心堂画室吗？</d>.

overall_soundscape: Old wooden floorboards creak faintly, a slow breeze stirs the pinned charcoal sketches on the wall, distant evening street noise drifts through the half-open lattice window, a wooden chair scrapes once.

non_diegetic_music: A low sparse cello drone over a single sustained piano note, slow and ominous, no clear rhythm.
```

```text
【镜头1】
```

### E01-02 · S01（黄昏） · 12 秒 · 3 个分镜

> 走位：来访者坐在画面左侧木椅上面朝右画师，两手夹膝；画师在右侧桌后，中间隔一张旧木桌。

| 切 | 起点 | 秒 | 景别 | 运镜 | 配方 | 画面 | 人物 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| #1 | 0.00s | 4.5 | 特写 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 周明：「我想让我的影子，替我去开周一的例会。」 | 周明 |
| #2 | 4.50s | 3 | 中景 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 沈砚拉过一把木椅，示意周明坐下。 沈砚：「坐。」 | 沈砚、周明 |
| #3 | 7.50s | 4.5 | 特写 | Push In（推） | — | 周明：「我在设计院画了三年图，署名全被人顶了。」 | 周明 |

```text
integrated_multimodal_description:

[Shot 2] At 00:04.500, the camera cuts to <Picture 2>: the painter pulls a wooden chair across and gestures for the visitor to sit, not raising his head, and utters a single flat word; a static shot; the camera holds a static shot (S2), a lean young painter in his late twenties, ink stains crawling up the back of his hand, flat cold even voice says <d>[Chinese]坐。</d>.
[Shot 3] At 00:07.500, the camera cuts to <Picture 3>: the seated man presses his hands between his knees and says he drew blueprints at the design institute for three years only to have his credit stolen; a slow push in toward his face; the camera performs a slow push in (S1) says <d>[Chinese]我在设计院画了三年图，署名全被人顶了。</d>.
```

### E01-03 · S01（黄昏） · 13.5 秒 · 3 个分镜

> 走位：正反打：来访者在左、画师在右，对话交替切近景；画师说到代价时双方对视。

| 切 | 起点 | 秒 | 景别 | 运镜 | 配方 | 画面 | 人物 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| #1 | 0.00s | 4.5 | 特写 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 周明：「十一个项目的奖金，一分钱都没到我手上。」 | 周明 |
| #2 | 4.50s | 3.5 | 中景 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 沈砚：「影子替你去，做你不敢做的事。」 | 沈砚 |
| #3 | 8.00s | 5.5 | 特写 | Push In（推） | — | 沈砚：「代价是永久剥掉你身上一项人格，长不回来。」 周明手指攥紧椅子边沿，指节泛白。 | 沈砚、周明 |

```text
[Shot 2] At 00:04.500, the camera cuts to <Picture 2>: the painter opens the sketchbook and states calmly that the shadow will do what he himself dares not; a static shot; the camera holds a static shot (S2), a lean young painter in his late twenties, ink stains crawling up the back of his hand, flat cold even voice says <d>[Chinese]影子替你去，做你不敢做的事。</d>.
[Shot 3] At 00:08.000, the camera cuts to <Picture 3>: the painter lifts his eyes and states the price is permanently stripping one piece of his personality, never to grow back; the visitor fingers clench the chair edge until the knuckles blanch; a slow push in; the camera performs a slow push in (S2) says <d>[Chinese]代价是永久剥掉你身上一项人格，长不回来。</d>.
```

> 走位：画师在右桌后把笔搁纸；来访者在左椅上闭眼点头；末镜俯拍两人之间地面，落在来访者脚边那团黑影。

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

| #2 | 4.50s | 4.5 | 中景 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 沈砚：「照心纹画出来才知道，我不挑。」 周明闭眼，喉头滚了一下，重重点头。 | 沈砚、周明 |
| #3 | 9.00s | 5.5 | 中景 | Push In（推） | — | 周明：「只要能把三年那口气出了，我签。」 沈砚合上速写本，指节在封面上轻叩三下。 周明低头看自己脚下，那团黑影比刚进门时沉了几分。 | 沈砚、周明 |

```text
[Shot 3] At 00:09.000, the camera cuts to <Picture 3>: the red-eyed man says he will sign just to vent three years of resentment; the painter closes the book and taps the cover three times; the visitor looks down at his own feet where the dark shadow has thickened; a slow push in toward the shadow; the camera performs a slow push in (S1) says <d>[Chinese]只要能把三年那口气出了，我签。</d>.
```

## E02

> 6 段 13 切 · 共 58.5 秒 / 目标 60 秒

### E02-01 · S01（黄昏） · 10.5 秒 · 2 个分镜

> 走位：来访者推门入画在左、走向木椅坐下；画师在右桌后，右手搭速写本，手背墨痕可见。

| 切 | 起点 | 秒 | 景别 | 运镜 | 配方 | 画面 | 人物 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| #1 | 0.00s | 5 | 中景 | Tracking Shot（跟拍） | — | 三天后黄昏，周明又推开画室门走进来，在木椅上坐下。 周明：「我想好了，我要离职。」 | 周明 |
| #2 | 5.00s | 5.5 | 特写 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 沈砚右手搭在速写本上，手背上一道墨痕在夕光里泛冷光。 周明：「十一个获奖项目，奖金全进了主管口袋。」 | 沈砚、周明 |

## E55

> 5 段 20 切 · 共 61 秒 / 目标 60 秒

### E55-01 · S01（夜戏） · 11.6 秒 · 4 个分镜

> 走位：画室夜。赵磊坐木椅偏右不动，沈砚在长桌后偏左持笔；闪回画面以剪影叠入。

| 切 | 起点 | 秒 | 景别 | 运镜 | 配方 | 画面 | 人物 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| #1 | 0.00s | 2.8 | 大特写 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 照心纹洇开，画室灯光被墨线吸暗了半秒。 | 沈砚 |
| #2 | 2.80s | 2.5 | 中景 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 赵磊坐在木椅上，眼前一段段画面浮起—— | 赵磊 |
| #3 | 5.30s | 2.8 | 全景 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 小学墙角，一个高他两头的男生伸手抢他手里的馒头。 | 赵磊 |
| #4 | 8.10s | 3.5 | 特写 | Push In（推） | — | 赵磊：「那个馒头，是我妈早上五点蒸的。」 | 赵磊 |

**H3 视频提示词**

```text
How the reference pictures align with the target video — Picture 1 (from Shot 1) aligns with the 0.00-second mark of the target video; Picture 2 (from Shot 2) aligns with the 2.80-second mark of the target video; Picture 3 (from Shot 3) aligns with the 5.30-second mark of the target video; Picture 4 (from Shot 4) aligns with the 8.10-second mark of the target video.

integrated_multimodal_description:
[Shot 1] A static shot holds on an extreme close-up; the ink pattern spreads across the paper and the studio light dims for half a second, drawn into the ink line.
[Shot 2] At 00:02.800, A static shot holds on a medium shot; the rider sits on the wooden chair, his gaze gone distant as memories rise before his eyes.
```

```text
[Shot 1] A wide tracking shot; the shadow walks down a street the woman had not dared turn down in ten years, stops before the iron visiting bars, and slides the thrice-wiped aluminum lunchbox through the gap.
```

```text
overall_soundscape: 街市声忽然发空，风停，极低频嗡鸣。

non_diegetic_music: 宏大的弦乐与管风琴，庄严悬疑。
```
